# Plugins · F · part 5

1,327 entries, forgeplan-orchestra to frontier-orchestrator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **forgeplan-orchestra**: Runs one tracked task end to end on whatever issue tracker a project uses — Orchestra, Jira, Linear, GitHub Issues — through seven gated stages, and carries the Orchestra MCP reference (field model, failure modes, query recipes) as a separate skill free of this ecosystem. Use when the board should tell the truth about work in flight: picking a task that can actually be started, keeping its checklist honest while the work happens, proving each acceptance line, and reporting what was NOT done alongside what was. Full history in CHANGELOG.md. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/forgeplan-orchestra) · ★4 · 3 skills, 2 commands, 2 agents, 1 hook file)
- **forgeplan-workflow**: Structured engineering workflow for forgeplan users: `/forge-cycle` drives one artifact through the full pipeline to activation with the quality gates enforced at each stage, and `/forge-audit` reviews what came out. Use when a piece of work is Standard depth or above and should not reach activation without evidence, a review and a gate — the cycle refuses to advance rather than letting a stage be skipped quietly. Full history in CHANGELOG.md. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/forgeplan-workflow) · ★4 · 1 skill, 2 commands, 1 agent, 1 hook file)
- **forgeproof**: Converts GitHub issues into working code with cryptographically signed provenance bundles. Every file edit, design decision, and test result is recorded in a tamper-evident Ed25519 hash chain. The .rpack bundle lets reviewers verify AI-generated code was not altered after creation — no external services, no API keys, pure local cryptography. ([ryanjmichie-git/forgeproof-plugin](https://github.com/ryanjmichie-git/forgeproof-plugin) · ★2 · 4 skills, 1 hook file)
- **forger**: Unattended development loop on GitHub Issues. Human entry points (/forger:setup, /forger:plan, /forger:work) and machine commands the loop drives (/forger:auto-ticket, /forger:auto-plan). ([diegoaltoworks/forger](https://github.com/diegoaltoworks/forger/tree/HEAD/plugins/forger) · 6 skills, 10 commands, 1 hook file)
- **forgestack**: Autonomous full-stack application factory for AI coding assistants. ([prematzerosoft/forgestack](https://github.com/prematzerosoft/forgestack) · 1 skill, 7 agents)
